<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$\ldots \times 40 = 280$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\ldots \times 40 = 280$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing  number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 40 &= 280 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac{280}{40} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac{7 \times 40}{40} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac{7 \times \cancel{40}}{\cancel{40}} \\\\
a           &= 7 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

$\ldots \div 30 = 5$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">

</div>
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing  number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \div 30       &= 5 \\\\
\dfrac{a}{30}   &= 5\\\\
a               &= 5 \times 30 \\\\
a               &= 150
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$8600 \div 20 = \ldots$
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8600 \div 20  \\\\
&= \dfrac {8600} {20}  \\\\
&= \dfrac {43 \times 2 \times 10 \times 10} {2 \times 10}  \\\\
&= \dfrac {43 \times \cancel{2} \times 10 \times 1\cancel{0} } { \cancel{2} \times 1\cancel{0} }  \\\\
&= 43 \times 10  \\\\
&= 430
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$200 \times \ldots = 160$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$200 \times \ldots = 160$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing number = $a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
200 \times a     &= 160 \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {160} {200} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {40 \times 4} {40 \times 5} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac { \cancel{40} \times 4} { \cancel{40} \times 5} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {4} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\dfrac {4} {5}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

There are $14$ boys in year $4$.

Given that $\dfrac{2}{5}$ of the year are boys, how many are girls?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">There are $14$ boys in year $4$.
<emptyline>
Given that $\dfrac{2}{5}$ of the year are boys, how many are girls?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the total number of students in year $4 = a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{2}{5} \times a                          &= 14  \\\\
\dfrac{2 \times a}{5}                          &= 14  \\\\
a                                              &= \dfrac{14 \times 5}{2} \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac{7 \times 2 \times 5}{2} \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac{7 \times \cancel{2} \times 5}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                               &= 7 \times 5 \\\\
                                               &= 35
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total children}   &= 35 \\\\
\text{Total girls}      &= \text{Total children} - \text{Total boys} \\\\      
                        &= 35 - 14  \\\\
                        &= 21
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$21$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the total number of students in year $4 = a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{2}{5} \times a                          &= 14  \\\\
\dfrac{2 \times a}{5}                          &= 14  \\\\
a                                              &= \dfrac{14 \times 5}{2} \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac{7 \times 2 \times 5}{2} \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac{7 \times \cancel{2} \times 5}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                               &= 7 \times 5 \\\\
                                               &= 35
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total children}   &= 35 \\\\
\text{Total girls}      &= \text{Total children} - \text{Total boys} \\\\      
                        &= 35 - 14  \\\\
                        &= 21
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$2a - b$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$2a - b$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: Using BIDMAS
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I am thinking of a number.

If I add $5$ to it and then divide by $2$, I get $6$. 

What number am I thinking of?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I am thinking of a number.
<emptyline>
If I add $5$ to it and then divide by $2$, I get $6$. 
<emptyline>
What number am I thinking of?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a + 5} { 2}    &= 6 \\\\
a + 5                  &= 6 \times 2 \\\\
a + 5                  &= 12 \\\\
a                      &= 12 - 5 \\\\
a                      &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

The number I am thinking of $= 7$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Solve the following equation to find $x$.

$5x - 7 = 43$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Solve the following equation to find $x$.
<emptyline>
$5x - 7 = 43$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
5x - 7         &= 43 \\\\
5x             &= 43 + 7 \\\\
5x             &= 50 \\\\
x              &= \dfrac {50} {5} \\\\
x              &= \dfrac {5 \times 10} {5} \\\\
x              &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 10} {\cancel 5} \\\\
x              &= 10
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$9 \times \ldots = 108$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
9 \times a             &= 108 \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {108} {9} \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {12 \times 9} {9} \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{9}} {\cancel{9}} \\\\
a                      &= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Gino is thinking of a number. 

If he

- halves it,
- then adds $5$ to it
- and then squares it and gets $121$.

Circle the number that Gino was thinking of.

$12 \quad 17 \quad 32$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Gino is thinking of a number. 
<emptyline>
If he
<emptyline>
- halves it,
- then adds $5$ to it
- and then squares it and gets $121$.
<emptyline>
Circle the number that Gino was thinking of.
<emptyline>
$12 \quad 17 \quad 32$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number Gino thought of $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\bigg(\dfrac{a}{2} + 5 \bigg)^2     &= 121 \\\\
\dfrac{a}{2} + 5                    &= \sqrt{121} \\\\
\dfrac{a}{2} + 5                    &= 11 \\\\
\dfrac{a}{2}                        &= 11 - 5 \\\\
a                                   &= 2 \times (11 - 5) \\\\
a                                   &= 2 \times 6 \\\\
a                                   &= 12 
\end{aligned}
$

The number that Gino thought of $= 12$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12$

</div>
</div>

|   Adults         |   $\pounds 6.25$ each |
|   Children       |   $\pounds 2.50$ each |

\end{aligned}
$

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The entry prices for a group are

|                  | Price                 |
|:-----------:     |:---------------------:|
|   Adults         |   $\pounds 5.00$ each |
|   Children       |   $\pounds 3.00$ each |
 
A group of adults and children goes to the theme park for a cost of $\pounds 44$.

There are $4$ adults in the group.
How many children are in the group? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The entry prices for a group are
<emptyline>
|                  | Price                 |
|:-----------:     |:---------------------:|
|   Adults         |   $\pounds 5.00$ each |
|   Children       |   $\pounds 3.00$ each |
<emptyline>
A group of adults and children goes to the theme park for a cost of $\pounds 44$.
<emptyline>
There are $4$ adults in the group.
How many children are in the group? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of children $= c$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times 5 + c \times 3       &= 44 \\\\
20 + 3c                       &= 44 \\\\
3c                            &= 44 - 20 \\\\
3c                            &= 24 \\\\
c                             &= \dfrac {24} {3} \\\\
c                             &= \dfrac {3 \times 8} {3} \\\\
c                             &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 8} {\cancel 3} \\\\
c                             &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

Number of children $= 8$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The total weight of one large and three small cans of Best Beans is $1.25 \ \text{kg}$. 
The large can weighs $425 \ \text{grams}$.

![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i00.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i00.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i00.png")
![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i01.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i01.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i01.png")

Work out the weight of one small can.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The total weight of one large and three small cans of Best Beans is $1.25 \ \text{kg}$. 
The large can weighs $425 \ \text{grams}$.
<emptyline>
%image%
%image%
<emptyline>
Work out the weight of one small can.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the weight of a small can (in $\text{grams}$) $= w$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total weight} &= 1.25 \ \text{kg}\\\\
                    &= 1.25 \times 1000 \ \text{grams} \\\\
                    &= 1250 \ \text{grams}
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
425 + 3w              &= 1250  \\\\
3w                    &= 1250 - 425  \\\\
3w                    &= 825  \\\\
w                     &= \dfrac{825}{3}  \\\\
w                     &= \dfrac{275 \times 3} {3}  \\\\
w                     &= \dfrac{275 \times \cancel 3} {\cancel 3}  \\\\
w                     &= 275 \ \text{grams}
\end{aligned}
$

Weight of a small can $= 275 \ \text{grams}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$275 \ \text{grams}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the weight of a small can (in $\text{grams}$) $= w$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total weight} &= 1.25 \ \text{kg}\\\\
                    &= 1.25 \times 1000 \ \text{grams} \\\\
                    &= 1250 \ \text{grams}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$

\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

There are $24$ pupils in $7\text{S}$.

Two thirds of them are boys.

Half of the boys have brown hair.

How many of pupils in $7\text{S}$ are boys with brown hair?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">There are $24$ pupils in $7\text{S}$.
<emptyline>
Two thirds of them are boys.
<emptyline>
Half of the boys have brown hair.
<emptyline>
How many of pupils in $7\text{S}$ are boys with brown hair?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of boys $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of boys}      &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 24 \\\\
a                          &= \dfrac {2 \times 24} {3} \\\\
a                          &= \dfrac {2 \times 8 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a                          &= \dfrac {2 \times 8 \times \cancel 3} {\cancel 3} \\\\
a                          &= 2 \times 8 \\\\
a                          &= 16
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Boys with brown hair}       &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times a\\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 16 \\\\
                                  &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If $2$ apples and $1$ banana cost $\pounds 1.20$ 

and

$1$ apple and $2$ bananas cost $\pounds 1.35$, find the cost of an apple and a banana separately.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If $2$ apples and $1$ banana cost $\pounds 1.20$ 
<emptyline>
and
<emptyline>
$1$ apple and $2$ bananas cost $\pounds 1.35$, find the cost of an apple and a banana separately.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of an apple $= a$

Let the cost of a banana $= b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned} 
2a + b &=  1.20 \\\\
a + 2b &= 1.35
\end{aligned}
$

We have got $2$ variables and we have $2$ equations to solve them. Let's eliminate to reduce it to just one variable. 

Multiply the second equation by $2$.

$
\begin{aligned} 
2 \times a + 2 \times 2b     &= 2 \times 1.35 \\\\
2a + 4b                      &= 2.70
\end{aligned}
$

Subtract the first equation from the second to reduce it to one variable.

$
\begin{aligned} 
2a + 4b - (2a + b)     &= 2.70 - 1.20 \\\\
2a + 4b -2a  -b        &= 1.50 \\\\
3b                     &= 1.50 \\\\
b                      &= \dfrac {1.50} {3} \\\\
b                      &= \pounds 0.50
\end{aligned}
$

Cost of a banana $= \pounds 0.50$

Let's substitute the value of $b$ in one of the initial equations to find the cost of an apple.

$
\begin{aligned} 
2a + b     &=  1.20 \\\\
2a + 50    &=  1.20 \\\\
2a         &=  1.20 - 0.50 \\\\
2a         &=  0.70 \\\\
a          &= \dfrac {0.70} {2} \\\\
a          &= \pounds 0.35
\end{aligned}
$

Cost of an apple $= \pounds 0.35$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Cost of an apple $= \pounds 0.35$

Cost of a banana $= \pounds 0.50$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>

</code></pre>
</div>

<emptyline>

**Question**

I am thinking of a number.

If I add $6$ then double it I get $46$.

What number am I thinking of?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I am thinking of a number.
<emptyline>
If I add $6$ then double it I get $46$.
<emptyline>
What number am I thinking of?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
(a + 6) \times 2            &= 46 \\\\
a + 6                       &= \dfrac {46} {2} \\\\
a + 6                       &= \dfrac {23 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a + 6                       &= \dfrac {23 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a + 6                       &= 23 \\\\
a                           &= 23 - 6 \\\\
a                           &= 17
\end{aligned}
$

The number I am thinking of $= 17$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Solve the following equation to find $x$.

$9x - 17 = 37$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Solve the following equation to find $x$.
<emptyline>
$9x - 17 = 37$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
9x - 17     &= 37 \\\\
9x          &= 37 + 17 \\\\
9x          &= 54 \\\\
x           &= \dfrac {54} {9} \\\\
x           &= \dfrac {9 \times 6} {9} \\\\
x           &= \dfrac {\cancel 9 \times 6} {\cancel 9} \\\\
x           &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Emilie is thinking of a number. If she

-  adds $2$ to it,
-  then squares it
-  and then subtracts $4$ she gets $60$.

Circle the number that Emilie was thinking of.

$4 \quad 6 \quad 30$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Emilie is thinking of a number. If she
<emptyline>
-  adds $2$ to it,
-  then squares it
-  and then subtracts $4$ she gets $60$.
<emptyline>
Circle the number that Emilie was thinking of.
<emptyline>
$4 \quad 6 \quad 30$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number Emilie thought of $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
(a + 2 ) ^ {2} - 4     &= 60 \\\\
(a + 2 ) ^ {2}         &= 60 + 4 \\\\
(a + 2 ) ^ {2}         &= 64 \\\\
a + 2                  &= \sqrt {64} \\\\
a + 2                  &= 8 \\\\
a                      &= 8 - 2 \\\\
a                      &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

Number that Emilie thought is $= 6$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$\dfrac{}{18} + \dfrac{1}{18} = \dfrac{1}{2}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\dfrac{}{18} + \dfrac{1}{18} = \dfrac{1}{2}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing number $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a} {18} + \dfrac {1} {18}     &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
\dfrac {a} {18} + \dfrac {1} {18}     &= \dfrac {1 \times 9} {2 \times 9} \\\\
\dfrac {a + 1} {18}                   &= \dfrac {9} {18} \\\\
a + 1                                 &= 9 \\\\
a                                     &= 9 - 1 \\\\
a                                     &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

$\dfrac{}{24} + \dfrac{1}{3} = \dfrac{1}{2}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\dfrac{}{24} + \dfrac{1}{3} = \dfrac{1}{2}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing number $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a} {24} + \dfrac {1} {3}                       &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
\dfrac {a} {24} + \dfrac {1 \times 8} {3 \times 8}     &= \dfrac {1 \times 12} {2 \times 12} \\\\
\dfrac {a} {24} + \dfrac {8} {24}                      &= \dfrac {12} {24} \\\\
\dfrac {a + 8} {24}                                    &= \dfrac {12} {24} \\\\
a + 8                                                  &= 12 \\\\
a                                                      &= 12 - 8 \\\\
a                                                      &= 4
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 0.4                &= 20 \\\\
a \times \dfrac {4} {10}    &= 20 \\\\

a                           &= \dfrac {4 \times 5 \times 10} {4} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 5 \times 10} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                           &= 5 \times 10  \\\\
a                           &= 50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Solve the following equation

$\dfrac{x}{2} = 10$


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Solve the following equation
<emptyline>
$\dfrac{x}{2} = 10$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {x} {2}  &= 10 \\\\
x               &= 10 \times 2 \\\\
x               &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Solve the following equation to find $b$.

$2b - 25 = 41$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Solve the following equation to find $b$.
<emptyline>
$2b - 25 = 41$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2b - 25     &= 41 \\\\
2b          &= 41 + 25 \\\\
2b          &= 66 \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {66} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {33 \times 2} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {33 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
b           &= 33
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$33$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
2b - 25     &= 41 \\\\
2b          &= 41 + 25 \\\\
2b          &= 66 \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {66} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {33 \times 2} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {33 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
b           &= 33
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Given that two teas and a coffee cost $ \pounds 2.50$ and one tea and three coffees cost $ \pounds 3.50$. 

How much does a single coffee cost?


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Given that two teas and a coffee cost $ \pounds 2.50$ and one tea and three coffees cost $ \pounds 3.50$. 
<emptyline>
How much does a single coffee cost?
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of a cup of tea $= t$

Let the cost of a cup of coffee $= c$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned} 
2t + c &= 2.50 \\\\
t + 3c &= 3.50
\end{aligned}
$

We have got $2$ variables and we have $2$ equations to solve them. Let's eliminate to reduce it to just one variable. 

Multiply the second equation by $2$.

$
\begin{aligned} 
2 \times t + 2 \times 3c &= 2 \times 3.50 \\\\
2t + 6c &= 7.00
\end{aligned}
$

Subtract the first equation from the second to reduce it to one variable.

$
\begin{aligned} 
(2t + 6c) - (2t + c) &= 7.00 - 2.50 \\\\
2t + 6c - 2t - c &= 4.50 \\\\
5c &= 4.50 \\\\
c &= \dfrac {4.50} {5} \\\\
c &= \pounds 0.90
\end{aligned}
$

Cost of a cup of coffee $= \pounds 0.90$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 0.90$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the cost of a cup of tea $= t$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>

\end{aligned}
$

\end{aligned}

<emptyline>

\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What fraction of ladies lost their gloves?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

                                                    &= \dfrac {5} {18}
\end{aligned}
$

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$52$ ladies did not lose their gloves.

Some ladies lost one glove and some lost both gloves. 

After the party, the $29$ missing gloves were found.

How many ladies lost only one glove?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$52$ ladies did not lose their gloves.
<emptyline>
Some ladies lost one glove and some lost both gloves. 
<emptyline>
After the party, the $29$ missing gloves were found.
<emptyline>
How many ladies lost only one glove?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Number of ladies who lost their gloves $= 72 - 52 = 20$

Let the ladies who lost only one glove $= a$

Ladies who lost both gloves $= 20 - a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + (20 - a) \times 2                 &= 29 \\\\
a + 40 - 2a                           &= 29 \\\\
a                              &= 40 - 29 \\\\
a                                     &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

Ladies who lost only one glove $= 11$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The sum of two consecutive whole numbers is $91$. 

What is the larger of these two numbers?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The sum of two consecutive whole numbers is $91$. 
<emptyline>
What is the larger of these two numbers?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the first number $= a$

Second number $= a + 1$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + (a + 1)               &= 91 \\\\
2 a + 1                 &= 91 \\\\
2 a                     &= 91 - 1 \\\\
2 a                     &= 90 \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {90} {2} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {45 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {45 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                       &= 45 \\\\
\text {Larger number}   &= a + 1 \\\\
                        &= 45 + 1 \\\\
                        &= 46
\end{aligned}
$ 
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$46$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

                        &= 46

$ 
</code></pre>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I thought of a number, doubled it and then subtracted $14$. 

I then divided the result by $3$ and got a final answer of $8$. 

What was the original number?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I thought of a number, doubled it and then subtracted $14$. 
<emptyline>
I then divided the result by $3$ and got a final answer of $8$. 
<emptyline>
What was the original number?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {2 a - 14} {3} &= 8 \\\\
2a - 14          &= 8 \times 3 \\\\
2a - 14          &= 24 \\\\
2a               &= 24 + 14 \\\\
2a               &= 38 \\\\
a                &= \dfrac {38} {2} \\\\
a                &= \dfrac {19 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                &= \dfrac {19 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                &= 19
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$19$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the number $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {2 a - 14} {3} &= 8 \\\\
2a - 14          &= 8 \times 3 \\\\
2a - 14          &= 24 \\\\
2a               &= 24 + 14 \\\\
2a               &= 38 \\\\
a                &= \dfrac {38} {2} \\\\
a                &= \dfrac {19 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                &= \dfrac {19 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                &= 19
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alison, Bhavini and Catherine share a bag of sweets.
Alison gets $5$ more sweets than Bhavini and Catherine gets twice as many sweets as Alison.

Catherine gets $22$ sweets. How many sweets are there in the bag?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alison, Bhavini and Catherine share a bag of sweets.
Alison gets $5$ more sweets than Bhavini and Catherine gets twice as many sweets as Alison.
<emptyline>
Catherine gets $22$ sweets. How many sweets are there in the bag?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of sweets Alison gets $= a$.

Bhavini gets $= a - 5$

Catherine gets $= 2 \times a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2a                    &= 22 \\\\
a                     &= \dfrac{22}{2} \\\\
a                     &= 11 \\\\
\text{Alison}         &= 11 \\\\
\text{Bhavini}        &= a - 5 \\\\
                      &= 11 - 5 \\\\
                      &= 6 \\\\
\text{Total sweets}   &= \text{Alison} + \text{Bhavini} + \text{Catherine} \\\\
                      &= 11 + 6 + 22 \\\\
                      &= 39
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$39$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the missing number.

$513 + \boxed{\phantom{3}} + 130 = 764$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the missing number.
<emptyline>
$513 + \boxed{\phantom{3}} + 130 = 764$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
513 + a + 130 &= 764 \\\\
a + 643       &= 764 \\\\
a             &= 764 - 643 \\\\
a             &= 121
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$121$

</div>
</div>

<div class="papername">
<p>dulwich-college--11-plus--maths--9999--specimen-paper-i:1:14</p>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A girl is $5\ \text{years}$ older than her brother. If the sum of their ages is $17$, how old are they?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A girl is $5\ \text{years}$ older than her brother. If the sum of their ages is $17$, how old are they?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let her brother's age $= a$

Girl's age $= a + 5$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 5   &= 17 \\\\
2a + 5      &= 17 \\\\
2a          &= 17 - 5 \\\\
2a          &= 12 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {12} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 6} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a           &= 6 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Brother's age}   &= a \\\\
                        &= 6 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Girl's age}      &= a + 5 \\\\
                        &= 6 + 5 \\\\
                        &= 11 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let her brother's age $= a$
<emptyline>
Girl's age $= a + 5$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 5   &= 17 \\\\
2a + 5      &= 17 \\\\
2a          &= 17 - 5 \\\\
2a          &= 12 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {12} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 6} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a           &= 6 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Brother's age}   &= a \\\\
                        &= 6 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Girl's age}      &= a + 5 \\\\
                        &= 6 + 5 \\\\
                        &= 11 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
